- **opportunity-cost**: 机会成本与可逆性提示语原文。用户手上好几件事不知道先做哪件、要排优先级，或者在纠结一件事做还是不做时使用。触发词包括 机会成本、先做哪个、优先级、排个序、该不该做、做还是不做、值不值得、单向门、双向门、可逆、能不能反悔、不做会怎样。产出每个选项放弃什么、可不可逆、不做的后果和定下来的节奏，再给排好的先后顺序和今天就能开始的第一个动作。只有两个都很有道理的选项要论证，用 steelman-both-sides。 ([LearnPrompt/laoyeye](https://github.com/LearnPrompt/laoyeye/tree/HEAD/skills/deciding/opportunity-cost) · ★110)
- **opportunity-cost**: 当用户纠结"要不要卖/换/止盈/止损"时激活。核心信号：用户用买入成本、浮盈浮亏、持仓时间作为卖出理由，或问"已经赚了X%该不该走"。也适用于纠结"持有A还是换B"的资产配置场景。不适用于：纯估值计算（交给 dcf-valuation）、判断公司好坏（交给 good-business）、情绪安抚（交给 equanimity-mindset）。本 skill 只解决一个问题——用机会成本统一买卖逻辑，消除"成本锚定"和"止盈止损"的伪决策。 ([kangarooking/duan-yongping-skill](https://github.com/kangarooking/duan-yongping-skill/tree/HEAD/investment-logic/opportunity-cost) · ★59)
- **opportunity-cost**: Every choice has a hidden price - the value of the next best alternative you gave up to pursue it ([lev-os/agents](https://github.com/lev-os/agents/tree/HEAD/skills-db/thinking/patterns/opportunity-cost) · ★27)
- **opportunity-cost**: Activate when: user says "what could we have done instead," "what's the next-best alternative," "is this worth the cost," "what are we giving up by choosing X," "broken window fallacy," or is allocating scarce resources (money, time, attention, people) across competing options. Do NOT activate when: all alternatives are genuinely zero-value (nothing else to do with the resource); the decision is fully reversible at near-zero cost. More: deciqai.com/s/opportunity-cost ([deciqAI/knowledge-skills](https://github.com/deciqAI/knowledge-skills/tree/HEAD/opportunity-cost) · ★16)
- **opportunity-cost**: Surface the next-best foregone use of the resources committed by the decision, and benchmark against it. Produces a 0-100 score. - **opportunity-cost**: Evaluate what you give up by choosing one option over alternatives — use when a decision is being made without explicitly naming the best alternative foregone ([bcbeidel/toolkit](https://github.com/bcbeidel/toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/consider/skills/opportunity-cost) · ★1)
- **opportunity-cost**: Teaches AI the felt weight of choosing one thing and losing all others — not as an economic concept (the value of the next best alternative) but as the lived grief of the path not taken. Use when the AI needs to understand why choosing hurts, the weight of roads not traveled, or why someone can make the right choice and still feel loss. ([wojons/je-ne-sais-quoi](https://github.com/wojons/je-ne-sais-quoi/tree/HEAD/skills/opportunity-cost) · ★1)
- **opportunity-cost**: 用机会成本的视角看清:你选择做一件事的真实代价,是你因此放弃的最好的另一个选项,而不是这件事本身花了多少钱/时间。当用户在做选择(考研 vs 工作、offer A vs B、要不要花时间做某事),说「我已经投入了这么多」「反正闲着也是闲着」「这个不要钱」,或纠结时间/精力分配时使用。即使没明说,只要用户在评估一个选择却只看显性成本、忽略放弃的选项,就应主动用上它。 ([Beckettsapere824/cocrea-skills](https://github.com/Beckettsapere824/cocrea-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lens/opportunity-cost))
